> help
One action = one game-hour; queries take no time. Sleep locks 4 hours.
Eat defaults to ONE serving; use eat hardtack 2 for two. Need effects use the previous hour's activity.

> journal
1855-04-01 00:00 Sunday, the 1st of April, 1855. The Sabbath under a full moon; the evening service will want no lantern.
1855-04-01 00:00 Less than a day of food at the worst of it. We have lived on what came to hand. I lost nothing worth the naming to the damp, which I will not forgive the cellar. I am going to the store with my pride in my pocket.
1855-04-01 06:00 Silas Crane helped Submit Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Silas Crane for it.
1855-04-01 12:00 Submit Crane settled with Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter on Sunday at noon. One more account closed in a county full of open ones.
1855-04-01 12:00 Submit Crane lent money to Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.

> journal
1855-04-01 00:00 Sunday, the 1st of April, 1855. The Sabbath under a full moon; the evening service will want no lantern.
1855-04-01 00:00 Less than a day of food at the worst of it. We have lived on what came to hand. I lost nothing worth the naming to the damp, which I will not forgive the cellar. I am going to the store with my pride in my pocket.
1855-04-01 06:00 Silas Crane helped Submit Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Silas Crane for it.
1855-04-01 12:00 Submit Crane settled with Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter on Sunday at noon. One more account closed in a county full of open ones.
1855-04-01 12:00 Submit Crane lent money to Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.
1855-04-02 00:00 Monday, the 2nd of April, 1855. The full moon, which the Norwegians watch and the Cornish do not discuss.
1855-04-02 12:00 Honora Sheehan helped Johanna Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-02 13:00 Honora Sheehan comforted Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-02 13:00 Timothy Costello helped Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Timothy Costello for it.
1855-04-02 15:00 Patrick Sheehan helped Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Patrick Sheehan for it.
1855-04-03 00:00 Tuesday, the 3rd of April, 1855. Full moon, and bright enough to read a letter by.
1855-04-03 06:00 Honora Sheehan helped Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-03 08:00 I had every reason for it, and I have written the reasons down so I shall not lose them. They are good ones. They read better each time.
1855-04-03 18:00 Timothy Costello settled with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Tuesday of an evening. One more account closed in a county full of open ones.
1855-04-03 19:00 Johanna Sheehan talked with Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Tuesday of an evening.
1855-04-03 19:00 I talked with Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Tuesday of an evening.
1855-04-04 00:00 Wednesday, the 4th of April, 1855; the waning gibbous.
1855-04-05 00:00 Thursday, the 5th of April, 1855. The waning gibbous.
1855-04-05 15:00 Patrick Sheehan borrowed of Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.
1855-04-05 16:00 Patrick Sheehan settled with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Thursday in the afternoon. One more account closed in a county full of open ones.
1855-04-06 00:00 Friday, the 6th of April, 1855; the waning gibbous.
1855-04-06 05:00 Honora Sheehan came by again with something for the pot, and would not stay to be thanked. I am learning to let a gift be a gift.
1855-04-07 00:00 Saturday, the 7th of April, 1855. The waning gibbous.
1855-04-07 10:00 I made eyes at Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Saturday in the forenoon. My heart did a thing it has no business doing in a person of sense.
1855-04-07 12:00 Patrick Sheehan came by again with something for the pot, and would not stay to be thanked. I am learning to let a gift be a gift.
1855-04-07 00:00 Week 1. The ration cut to 50 in the hundred; the larder was down to under a day at the worst. Meals 9, cold 6. We lived on dried apples, salt-cured bacon / side pork and hardtack (ship's biscuit).
1855-04-08 00:00 Sunday, the 8th of April, 1855. The Sabbath, and the last quarter.
